Vilom Shabd (Hindi Antonyms): Meaning, Types, Rules, Examples & Important Words for Competitive Exams

Vilom Shabd: Definition, Rules, Classification & 1,620 Revised Word Pairs

🔍

Vilom Shabd: Definition, Rules, Grammatical Category, and Formation Process

Literal meaning of 'Vilom Shabd' (Antonym)
A word that conveys an opposite or reverse meaning (Ulta ya viparit arth dene wala)
Tap to view answer
Other common names for Vilom Shabd in Hindi grammar
Viparitathak Shabd or Pratilom Shabd (Opposite-meaning words)
Tap to view answer
Supreme grammatical rule for determining a Vilom Shabd (Antonym)
It must belong to the same grammatical category (Saman vyakaranik koti)
Tap to view answer
In what form must the antonym of a Tatsam (Sanskrit-origin) word always be?
In Tatsam form
Tap to view answer
In what form must the antonym of a Tadbhav (native Hindi) word always be?
In Tadbhav form
Tap to view answer
To which grammatical class does the antonym of a Noun (Sangya) belong?
Noun (Sangya)
Tap to view answer
To which grammatical class does the antonym of an Adjective (Visheshan) belong?
Adjective (Visheshan)
Tap to view answer
To which grammatical class does the antonym of a Verb (Kriya) belong?
Verb (Kriya)
Tap to view answer
To which grammatical class does the antonym of an Adverb (Kriyavisheshan) belong?
Adverb (Kriyavisheshan)
Tap to view answer
What are antonyms formed by adding a prefix (Upsarga) called?
Prefix-derived antonyms (Upsarga-nirmit vilom)
Tap to view answer
Example of forming an antonym by adding the prefix 'A-' (अ)
Asatya (असत्य — Falsehood) from Satya (सत्य — Truth)
Tap to view answer
Example of forming an antonym by adding the prefix 'An-' (अन)
Anuchit (अनुचित — Improper) from Uchit (उचित — Proper)
Tap to view answer
Example of forming an antonym by adding the prefix 'Ap-' (अप)
Apaman (अपमान — Insult) from Maan (मान — Honor)
Tap to view answer
Antonym formed using the prefix 'Dur-' (दुर्) or 'Dush-' (दुष्)
Durjan (दुर्जन — Wicked) from Sajjan (सज्जन — Gentleman)
Tap to view answer
Antonym formed using the prefix 'Nir-' (निर्) or 'Nish-' (निष्)
Nishkriya (निष्क्रिय — Inactive) from Sakriya (सक्रिय — Active)
Tap to view answer
Antonym formed by adding the prefix 'Vi-' (वि)
Virag (विराग — Detachment) from Raag (राग — Attachment)
Tap to view answer
Antonym formed by adding the prefix 'Prati-' (प्रति)
Prativadi (प्रतिवादी — Defendant) from Vadi (वादी — Plaintiff)
Tap to view answer
Example of forming an antonym through gender change (Ling parivartan)
Rani (रानी — Queen) from Raja (राजा — King)
Tap to view answer
Antonyms formed from completely independent, distinct root words
Pure/Complete Antonyms (Purna Vilom — e.g., Raat [रात — Night] from Din [दिन — Day])
Tap to view answer
What error occurs when using synonyms instead of exact antonyms?
Nuance and grammatical-category error (Arthachhata aur koti dosh)
Tap to view answer
Exact Tatsam antonym of 'Andhkar' (अंधकार — Darkness)
Prakash (प्रकाश — Light)
Tap to view answer
Exact Tadbhav antonym of 'Andhera' (अंधेरा — Darkness)
Ujala (उजाला — Light/Brightness)
Tap to view answer
Exact classical antonym of 'Mook' (मूक — Mute)
Vachal (वाचाल — Talkative)
Tap to view answer
Famous competitive-exam antonym of 'Sthavar' (स्थावर — Immovable)
Jangam (जंगम — Movable)
Tap to view answer
What increases in language through proper mastery of antonyms?
Vocabulary and expressive proficiency (Shabd-bhandar evam abhivyakti-dakshata)
Tap to view answer
